1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
    14→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    15→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path
    18→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    19→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    20→from desloppify.state import load_state
    21→
    22→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    25→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    26→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    27→
    28→
    29→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    30→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    31→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    32→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    33→        from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names
    34→
    35→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    36→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    37→
    38→def create_parser():
    39→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    40→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    41→
    42→
    43→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    44→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    45→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    46→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    47→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    48→    if not combined:
    49→        return
    50→    set_exclusions(combined)
    51→    if cli_exclusions:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    54→            file=sys.stderr,
    55→        )
    56→        return
    57→    print(
    58→        colorize(
    59→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    60→        ),
    61→        file=sys.stderr,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    66→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    67→
    68→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    69→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    70→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    71→    """
    72→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    73→        return
    74→    runtime_root = (
    75→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    76→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    77→        else get_project_root()
    78→    )
    79→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    80→        try:
    81→            state_file = state_path(args)
    82→            if state_file:
    83→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    84→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    85→                if saved_path:
    86→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    87→                    return
    88→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    89→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    90→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    91→    if lang:
    92→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
    93→    else:
    94→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
    95→
    96→
    97→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    98→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
    99→    config = load_config()
   100→
   101→    state_file = state_path(args)
   102→    state = load_state(state_file)
   103→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   104→
   105→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   109→    from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
   110→
   111→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   112→
   113→
   114→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   115→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   116→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   117→    try:
   118→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   119→    except SystemExit:
   120→        return
   121→
   122→
   123→def main() -> None:
   124→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   125→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   126→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   127→            try:
   128→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   129→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   130→                logger.debug(
   131→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   132→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   133→                )
   134→
   135→    parser = create_parser()
   136→    args = parser.parse_args()
   137→    if args.command == "help":
   138→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    try:
   142→        with runtime_scope():
   143→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   144→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   145→
   146→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   147→            handler(args)
   148→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   149→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   150→        sys.exit(1)
   151→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   152→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   153→        sys.exit(1)
   154→
   155→
   156→if __name__ == "__main__":
   157→    main()
   158→

1→"""CLI parser group builders for admin/workflow command families."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.languages import get_lang
     9→
    10→
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    30→
    31→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, True)
    37→
    38→

    40→    p_detect = sub.add_parser(

    49→    p_detect.add_argument(
    50→        "--fix",

    52→        help="Auto-fix detected issues (logs detector only)",

    54→    p_detect.add_argument(
    55→        "--category",
    56→        choices=["imports", "vars", "params", "all"],

    66→    p_detect.add_argument(
    67→        "--file", type=str, default=None, help="Show deps for specific file"

    69→    p_detect.add_argument(

    95→    p_review.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)

    97→    p_review.add_argument(

   394→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   395→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   396→    for lang_name in langs:
   397→        try:
   398→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   399→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   400→            fixer_names = []
   401→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   402→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   403→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   404→    return fixer_help_lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   408→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   409→        "fix",
   410→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   411→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   412→    )
   413→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   414→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
   415→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
   416→    p_fix.add_argument(
   417→        "--dry-run",
   418→        action="store_true",
   419→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   420→    )
   421→

1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   125→
```

    67→  desloppify move scripts/foo/bar.py scripts/foo/baz/bar.py
    68→"""
    69→
    70→
    71→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    72→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    73→
    74→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    75→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    76→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    77→
    78→
    79→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    80→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    81→    try:
    82→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    83→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    84→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    85→
    86→
    87→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    88→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    89→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    90→
    91→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    92→        prog="desloppify",
    93→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    94→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    95→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    96→    )
    97→    parser.add_argument(
    98→        "--lang",
    99→        type=str,
   100→        default=None,
   101→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   102→    )
   103→    parser.add_argument(
   104→        "--exclude",
   105→        action="append",
   106→        default=None,
   107→        metavar="PATTERN",
   108→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   109→    )
   110→    parser.add_argument(
   111→        "--version",
   112→        "-V",
   113→        action="version",
   114→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   115→    )
   116→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   117→        dest="command",
   118→        required=True,
   119→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   120→    )
   121→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_resolve_parser(sub)
   127→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   129→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   130→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   132→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   135→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   137→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   138→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   139→    return parser
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   143→

  tree                          Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
  show <pattern>                Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
  resolve <pattern> <status>    Mark findings as open/fixed/wontfix/false_positive
  ignore <pattern>              Suppress findings matching a pattern
  zone show                     Show zone classifications for all files
  zone set <file> <zone>        Override zone for a file
  review --prepare              Prepare holistic codebase review data
  review --import FILE          Import review findings from JSON
  review --validate-import FILE Validate import payload/trust mode without mutating state
  review --external-start       Start external blind review session (Claude cloud)
  review --external-submit      Submit external session results with canonical provenance
  review --merge                Merge duplicate review findings
  plan                          Generate prioritized markdown plan

examples:
  desloppify scan --skip-slow
  desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
  desloppify tree --focus shared/components --sort findings --depth 3
```

                        phrases 'without awareness' and 'unbiased'.
  --max-age MAX_AGE     Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)

  --external-submit     Submit external reviewer JSON via a started session;

  --runner {codex}      Subagent runner backend (default: codex)
  --parallel            Run selected batches in parallel

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me create a comprehensive summary report:

## CLI Help and Discoverability System - Comprehensive Analysis

Based on a thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete picture of how the desloppify CLI's help system is structured:

---

### **1. Argparse Architecture Overview**

**Entry Points:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/cli.py` - Main entry point with `main()` function
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py` - Core parser builder
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` - Re-exports from `parser_groups_admin.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` - Individual command parser builders (11 functions)

**Key Parser Customization:**
- Custom `_NoAbbrevArgumentParser` class disables long-option abbreviation (`allow_abbrev=False`)
- Uses `argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` for proper formatting of epilog sections
- Top-level parser marked with `required=True` on subcommands

---

### **2. Top-Level Commands (18 Total)**

When you run `desloppify --help`, all 18 commands are listed:

| Command | Help Text | Type |
|---------|-----------|------|
| `scan` | Run all detectors, update state, show diff | Workflow |
| `status` | Score dashboard with per-tier progress | Workflow |
| `tree` | Annotated codebase tree (text) | Workflow |
| `show` | Dig into findings by file, directory, detector, or ID | Workflow |
| `next` | Show next highest-priority open finding | Workflow |
| `resolve` | Mark finding(s) as fixed/wontfix/false_positive/open | Workflow |
| `ignore` | Add pattern to ignore list, remove matching findings | Workflow |
| `fix` | Auto-fix mechanical issues | Workflow |
| `plan` | Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note | Workflow |
| `viz` | Generate interactive HTML treemap | Workflow |
| `detect` | Run a single detector directly (bypass state) | Admin |
| `move` | Move a file or directory and update all import references | Admin |
| `review` | Prepare or import holistic subjective review | Admin |
| `zone` | Show/set/clear zone classifications | Admin |
| `config` | Show/set/unset project configuration | Admin |
| `dev` | Developer utilities | Admin |
| `langs` | List all available language plugins with depth and tools | Admin |
| `update-skill` | Install or update the desloppify skill/agent document | Admin |

---

### **3. Global Options (3 Total)**

Available on all commands:

```
--lang LANG           Language to scan (auto-detected if omitted)
--exclude PATTERN     Path pattern to exclude (repeatable)
--version, -V         Show version
-h, --help           Show help
```

---

### **4. Subcommand Hierarchy - The `plan` Command Family**

The `plan` command is the most complex, with 11 subcommands + nested cluster subcommands:

**Direct `plan` Subcommands (11):**
1. `plan show` - Show plan metadata summary
2. `plan reset` - Reset plan to empty
3. `plan move` - Move findings in the queue
4. `plan describe` - Set augmented description
5. `plan note` - Set note on findings
6. `plan skip` - Skip findings (temp/permanent/false positive)
7. `plan unskip` - Bring skipped findings back
8. `plan done` - Mark findings as fixed (purges from plan)
9. `plan reopen` - Reopen resolved findings
10. `plan focus` - Set or clear active cluster focus
11. `plan cluster` - Manage finding clusters (nested)

**Nested `plan cluster` Subcommands (7):**
- `plan cluster create` - Create a cluster
- `plan cluster add` - Add findings to cluster
- `plan cluster remove` - Remove findings from cluster
- `plan cluster delete` - Delete a cluster
- `plan cluster move` - Move cluster as a block
- `plan cluster show` - Show cluster details
- `plan cluster list` - List all clusters

**Plan-level Options:**
```
--state STATE         Path to state file
--output FILE         Write to file instead of stdout
```

**Full help for `plan cluster create` example:**
```
usage: desloppify plan cluster create [-h] [--description DESCRIPTION]
                                      [--action ACTION]
                                      cluster_name

positional arguments:
  cluster_name          Cluster name (slug)

options:
  --description DESCRIPTION    Cluster description
  --action ACTION              Primary action/command for this cluster
```

---

### **5. Commands with Epilog/Dynamic Help Text**

**`detect` command:** Epilog lists all 45+ available detectors
```
detectors: boilerplate_duplication, cargo_error, clippy_warning, 
clj_kondo_finding, concerns, coupling, cppcheck_finding, credo_issue, 
cycles, deprecated, dialyzer_warning, dict_keys, dupes, eslint_warning, 
exports, facade, flat_dirs, fsharp_error, global_mutable_config, 
hlint_suggestion, ... (45+ total)
```

**`fix` command:** Epilog lists all available fixers by language
```
fixers (bash): none yet
fixers (clojure): none yet
fixers (javascript): eslint-warning
fixers (kotlin): ktlint-violation
fixers (python): none yet
fixers (ruby): rubocop-offense
fixers (rust): clippy-warning
fixers (typescript): dead-useeffect, debug-logs, empty-if-chain, 
                     unused-imports, unused-params, unused-vars
fixers (zig): none yet
special: review — prepare structured review data
```

---

### **6. Help with Examples**

The main `--help` output includes a **USAGE_EXAMPLES** section at the bottom (defined in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py` lines 29-68):

**Workflow examples section** showcases:
- `scan`, `status`, `tree`, `show`, `resolve`, `ignore`, `zone`, `review`, `plan`

**Practical examples** including:
```
desloppify scan --skip-slow
desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
desloppify tree --focus shared/components --sort findings --depth 3
desloppify show src/shared/components/PromptEditorModal.tsx
desloppify resolve fixed "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \
  --note "removed unused React import" \
  --attest "I have actually..."
desloppify detect logs --top 10
desloppify review --validate-import findings.json --attested-external
desloppify dev scaffold-lang go --extension .go --marker go.mod
desloppify move src/shared/hooks/useFoo.ts src/shared/hooks/video/useFoo.ts
```

---

### **7. Help Customization Features**

**Parser Customizations:**
- `allow_abbrev=False` - Prevents abbreviating long options (strict matching)
- `RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` - Preserves formatting in epilogs
- Custom `_DeprecatedAction` and `_DeprecatedBoolAction` classes for handling deprecated options

**Deprecated Options Example (in `review`):**
```python
p_review.add_argument("--max-age", ..., action=_DeprecatedAction, ...)
# Prints: "Warning: --max-age is deprecated and will be removed..."
```

---

### **8. Command Handler Resolution**

Commands are dispatched via a centralized registry in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/registry.py`:

```python
_COMMAND_HANDLERS = {
    "scan": cmd_scan,
    "status": cmd_status,
    "show": cmd_show,
    "next": cmd_next,
    "resolve": cmd_resolve,
    "ignore": cmd_ignore_pattern,
    "fix": cmd_fix,
    "plan": cmd_plan,  # <-- Entry point for all plan subcommands
    "detect": cmd_detect,
    "tree": cmd_tree,
    "viz": cmd_viz,
    "move": cmd_move,
    "zone": cmd_zone,
    "review": cmd_review,
    "config": cmd_config,
    "dev": cmd_dev,
    "langs": cmd_langs,
    "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
}
```

**Plan Command Dispatch:**
The `cmd_plan()` function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py` dispatches to:
- `_cmd_plan_generate()` when no subcommand given (backwards compatible, generates markdown)
- `_cmd_plan_show()`, `_cmd_plan_reset()`, `cmd_plan_move()`, etc. based on `plan_action`
- `cmd_cluster_dispatch()` for cluster subcommands
- `cmd_plan_override_handlers` module for skip/unskip/done/reopen/describe/note/focus

### **Key Insights for Help System Design**

1. **Two-tier dispatch:** CLI args → command handlers → subcommand handlers (for plan)
2. **Decentralized parsing:** Each command's parser built by dedicated `_add_X_parser()` function
3. **Dynamic content:** Detector names and fixer lists computed at parser creation time
4. **Discoverability:** Examples section + epilogs supplement argparse defaults
5. **Anti-gaming:** Help text prominently features attestation requirements
6. **Backwards compatibility:** Plan command auto-generates markdown when no subcommand given

This system balances comprehensive help (examples, detector lists, fixer availability) with strict argparse discipline (no abbreviations, required subcommands, explicit option validation).

    70→"""
    71→
    72→
    73→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    74→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    75→
    76→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    77→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    78→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    82→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    83→    try:
    84→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    85→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    86→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    87→
    88→
    89→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    90→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    91→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    92→
    93→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    94→        prog="desloppify",
    95→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    96→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    97→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    98→    )
    99→    parser.add_argument(
   100→        "--lang",
   101→        type=str,
   102→        default=None,
   103→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   104→    )
   105→    parser.add_argument(
   106→        "--exclude",
   107→        action="append",
   108→        default=None,
   109→        metavar="PATTERN",
   110→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   111→    )
   112→    parser.add_argument(
   113→        "--version",
   114→        "-V",
   115→        action="version",
   116→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   117→    )
   118→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   119→        dest="command",
   120→        required=True,
   121→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   122→    )
   123→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   127→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_resolve_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   130→    _add_exclude_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   132→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   135→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   137→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   138→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   139→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   140→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   141→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   142→    return parser
   143→
   144→
   145→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   146→
```

    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    30→
    31→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, True)

   394→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   395→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   396→    for lang_name in langs:
   397→        try:
   398→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   399→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   400→            fixer_names = []
   401→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   402→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   403→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   404→    return fixer_help_lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   408→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   409→        "fix",
   410→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   411→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),

   414→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)

   416→    p_fix.add_argument(
   417→        "--dry-run",
   418→        action="store_true",
   419→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   420→    )
   421→

   494→    p_skip.add_argument(

   496→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",

   498→
   499→    # plan unskip <patterns>

   169→    p_next.add_argument(
   170→        "--status",
   171→        choices=["open", "fixed", "wontfix", "false_positive", "auto_resolved", "all"],
   172→        default="open",
   173→        help="Status filter for queue items (default: open)",

   175→    p_next.add_argument(

   177→        choices=["item", "file", "detector", "tier"],

   186→    )
   187→    p_next.add_argument(

   217→def _add_resolve_parser(sub) -> None:

   249→    p_resolve.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)

1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   125→

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    if config.get("needs_rescan"):
    76→        print(colorize("  Config changed — scores may be stale. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    82→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    83→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    84→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    85→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    86→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    87→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    88→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    89→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    90→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    91→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    92→
    93→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    94→
    95→    # Load the living plan
    96→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    97→    plan = load_plan()
    98→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    99→    if (
   100→        plan.get("queue_order")
   101→        or plan.get("overrides")
   102→        or plan.get("clusters")
   103→    ):
   104→        plan_data = plan
   105→
   106→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   107→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   108→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   109→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   110→        if active_cluster:
   111→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   112→
   113→    queue = build_work_queue(
   114→        state,
   115→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   116→            tier=tier,
   117→            count=count,
   118→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   119→            scope=scope,
   120→            status=status,
   121→            include_subjective=True,
   122→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   123→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   124→            explain=explain,
   125→            plan=plan_data,
   126→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   127→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   128→        ),
   129→    )
   130→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   131→
   132→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   133→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   134→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   135→        state,
   136→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   140→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   141→    )
   142→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   143→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   144→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   145→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   146→        state,
   147→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   148→    )
   149→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   150→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   151→    ]
   152→    write_query(payload)
   153→
   154→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   155→    if output_file:
   156→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   157→            output_file,
   158→            payload,
   159→            len(items),
   160→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   161→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   162→        ):
   163→            return
   164→        raise SystemExit(1)
   165→
   166→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   170→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   171→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   175→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   176→        state.get("findings", {}),
   177→        state.get("scan_path"),
   178→    )
   179→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   180→    try:
   181→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   182→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   183→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   184→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   185→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   186→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   187→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   188→    )
   189→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   190→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   191→        state,
   192→        dim_scores,
   193→        findings_scoped,
   194→        strict_score=strict_score,
   195→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   196→    )
   197→    print()
   198→
   199→
   200→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   201→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   339→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   340→        return
   341→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   342→    if not explain:
   343→        return
   344→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   345→    if available:
   346→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   348→
   349→
   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   355→    if tier is not None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(
   360→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True

   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,
   369→    findings_scoped: dict,
   370→    *,
   371→    group: str,
   372→    explain: bool,
   373→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   374→    plan: dict | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→
   401→
   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   431→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   432→    )
   433→    print(
   434→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→

    42→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    43→    """Show score dashboard."""
    44→    runtime = command_runtime(args)

    46→    config = runtime.config
    47→

    52→    suppression = state_mod.suppression_metrics(state)
    53→
    54→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    55→        print(

    62→                    subjective_measures,
    63→                    suppression,
    64→                ),
    65→                indent=2,
    66→            )
    67→        )
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    74→    if stale_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    77→    if skill_warning:
    78→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    79→    if config.get("needs_rescan"):
    80→        print(colorize("  Config changed — scores may be stale. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    81→
    82→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    83→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
    84→    target_strict_score = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    85→
    86→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    87→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    88→
    89→    # Load living plan for plan-aware rendering and narrative
    90→    _plan = load_plan()

    92→        _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
    93→    ) else None
    94→

    96→        state,
    97→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="status", plan=_plan_active),
    98→    )
    99→    ignores = config.get("ignore", [])
   100→
   101→    for line, style in score_summary_lines(

   146→    write_status_query(

    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"

    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,

    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",

   100→            "objective_score": objective_score,

   108→            "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
   109→            "by_tier": by_tier,

   116→            "next_command": _status_next_command(narrative),

   129→def show_ignore_summary(ignores: list[str], suppression: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Show ignore list plus suppression accountability from recent scans."""
   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))
   132→    for p in ignores[:10]:

   137→    last_pct = float(suppression.get("last_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   138→
   139→    if last_raw > 0:
   140→        style = "red" if last_pct >= 30 else "yellow" if last_pct >= 10 else "dim"
   141→        print(

   144→                style,

   147→    elif suppression.get("recent_scans", 0):

   149→
   150→    recent_scans = int(suppression.get("recent_scans", 0) or 0)
   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)
   155→        print(

   162→
   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,
   167→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )

   290→def _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective: list[dict], state: dict | None = None) -> None:
   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(
   293→        colorize("  Health = open penalized | Strict = open + wontfix penalized", "dim")
   294→    )
   295→    print(
   296→        colorize(

   298→            "dim",

   300→    )
   301→    stale_keys = [
   302→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   303→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   304→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   305→    ]
   306→    if stale_keys:
   307→        n = len(stale_keys)
   308→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   309→        print(
   310→            colorize(
   311→                f"  [stale] = subjective assessment outdated, not an unresolved finding"
   312→                f" — re-review to refresh",
   313→                "yellow",
   314→            )
   315→        )
   316→        print(
   317→            colorize(
   318→                f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   319→                f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`",
   320→                "yellow",
   321→            )
   322→        )

   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→
