```
   492→
   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→
   587→
   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:
   597→        return
   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )
   601→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   602→    if uninvestigated:
   603→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   607→        print(
   608→            colorize(
   609→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   610→                "dim",
   611→            )
   612→        )
   613→    print()
   614→
```

```
1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    21→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    22→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    23→    state = runtime.state
    24→
    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    26→        return
    27→
    28→    if runtime.config.get("needs_rescan"):
    29→        print(colorize("  Config changed — scores may be stale. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    30→
    31→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    32→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    33→
    34→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    35→    if output:
    36→        try:
    37→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    38→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    39→            print_agent_plan(
    40→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    41→                next_command=next_command,
    42→            )
    43→        except OSError as e:
    44→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    45→    else:
    46→        print(plan_md)
    47→        print()
    48→        print_agent_plan(
    49→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    50→            next_command=next_command,
    51→        )
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    55→
```

```
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (

    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None

    28→        header_score = (

    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"

    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→

    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",

    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",

    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(

    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",

    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {

    83→    }
    84→

    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→

    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)

   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )

   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows

   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def _plan_user_ordered_section(
   292→    items: list[dict],
   293→    plan: dict,
   294→) -> list[str]:
   295→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
   296→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   297→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   298→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   299→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   300→
   301→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
   302→    if not ordered_ids:
   303→        return []
   304→
   305→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   306→
   307→    lines: list[str] = [
   308→        "---",
   309→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
   310→        "",
   311→    ]
   312→
   313→    # Group by cluster: clustered items first, then unclustered
   314→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   315→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   316→        member_ids = [
   317→            fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   318→            if fid in ordered_ids and fid in by_id
   319→        ]
   320→        if not member_ids:
   321→            continue
   322→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   323→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   324→        if desc:
   325→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   326→        lines.append("")
   327→        for fid in member_ids:
   328→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   329→            if item:
   330→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   331→                emitted.add(fid)
   332→        lines.append("")
   333→
   334→    # Unclustered ordered items
   335→    unclustered = [
   336→        fid for fid in queue_order
   337→        if fid in ordered_ids and fid not in emitted and fid in by_id
   338→    ]
   339→    if unclustered:
   340→        if any(c.get("finding_ids") for c in clusters.values()):
   341→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   342→            lines.append("")
   343→        for fid in unclustered:
   344→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   345→            if item:
   346→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   347→        lines.append("")
   348→
   349→    return lines
   350→
   351→
   352→def _plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   353→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   354→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   355→    if not skipped:
   356→        return []
   357→
   358→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   359→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   360→
   361→    # Group by kind
   362→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   363→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   364→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   365→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(fid)
   366→
   367→    kind_labels = {
   368→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   369→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   370→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    lines: list[str] = [
   374→        "---",
   375→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   376→        "",
   377→    ]
   378→
   379→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   380→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   381→        if not ids:
   382→            continue
   383→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   384→        lines.append("")
   385→        for fid in ids:
   386→            entry = skipped.get(fid, {})
   387→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   388→            if item:
   389→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   390→            else:
   391→                lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~ (not in current queue)")
   392→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   393→            if reason:
   394→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   395→            note = entry.get("note")
   396→            if note and not overrides.get(fid, {}).get("note"):
   397→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   398→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   399→            if review_after:
   400→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   401→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   402→        lines.append("")
   403→
   404→    return lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   409→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   410→    if not superseded:
   411→        return []
   412→
   413→    lines: list[str] = [
   414→        "---",
   415→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   416→        "",
   417→    ]
   418→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   419→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   420→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   421→        lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~{summary_str}")
   422→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   423→        if candidates:
   424→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   425→        note = entry.get("note")
   426→        if note:
   427→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   428→    lines.append("")
   429→    return lines
   430→
   431→
   432→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   433→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   434→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   435→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   436→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   437→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   438→
   439→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   440→    desc = override.get("description")
   441→    if desc:
   442→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   443→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   444→    note = override.get("note")
   445→    if note:
   446→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   447→    return lines
   448→
   449→
   450→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   451→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   452→
   453→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   454→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   455→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   456→    """
   457→    findings = state["findings"]
   458→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   459→
   460→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   461→    if plan is None:
   462→        try:
   463→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   464→            plan = load_plan()
   465→        except Exception:
   466→            plan = {}
   467→
   468→    has_plan = bool(
   469→        plan
   470→        and (
   471→            plan.get("queue_order")
   472→            or plan.get("skipped")
   473→            or plan.get("clusters")
   474→        )
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   478→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   479→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   480→
   481→    if has_plan:
   482→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   483→        queue = build_work_queue(
   484→            state,
   485→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   486→                count=None,
   487→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   488→                status="open",
   489→                include_subjective=True,
   490→                no_tier_fallback=True,
   491→            ),
   492→        )
   493→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   494→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   495→
   496→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   497→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   498→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   499→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   500→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   501→        if remaining:
   502→            lines.append("---")
   503→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   504→            lines.append("")
   505→
   506→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   507→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   508→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   509→    else:
   510→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   511→
   512→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   513→
   514→    return "\n".join(lines)
   515→
```

```
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
```

```
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""

   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):

   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:

   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)

   200→    # Build cluster meta-items

   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
```

## Complete Parser Infrastructure Analysis

### Overview

The CLI parser system is organized in three main files:

1. **`parser.py`** - Top-level parser builder with the main `create_parser()` function
2. **`parser_groups.py`** - Re-export hub that aggregates parser builders
3. **`parser_groups_admin.py`** - Actual implementation of all `_add_*_parser()` functions

### Key Structural Findings

#### 1. **Parser Creation Pattern**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py` (lines 73-142)

- Uses custom `_NoAbbrevArgumentParser` class (line 73-78) that disables long-option abbreviation via `allow_abbrev=False`
- Top-level parser uses `argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` to preserve epilog formatting (line 97)
- Epilog is set to `USAGE_EXAMPLES` constant (lines 30-70)
- Top-level arguments: `--lang`, `--exclude`, `--version`
- Subparsers created with `add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)` (lines 118-122)

#### 2. **Parser Grouping & Organization**

**Pattern:**
- Each major command gets its own `_add_*_parser(sub)` function that:
  1. Calls `sub.add_parser(name, help="...", epilog=...)`
  2. Adds arguments via `add_argument()`
  3. For commands with subcommands, calls `add_subparsers(dest="...")` to create nested parsers

**Hierarchy of Commands:**
- **Simple subcommands** (single level): scan, status, tree, show, next, resolve, ignore, exclude, fix, viz, dev, langs, update-skill
- **Commands with subcommands** (two levels): plan, zone, config, review

#### 3. **Argparse Argument Groups**

**Status:** NOT USED ANYWHERE. The code uses simple sequential `add_argument()` calls without creating explicit argument groups via `add_argument_group()`.

#### 4. **Epilog Formatting**

**Approach:**
- Uses `RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` to preserve line breaks and formatting
- Epilogs are embedded in individual `_add_*_parser()` calls:
  - Line 43: `_add_detect_parser()` uses epilog with comma-separated detector names
  - Line 411: `_add_fix_parser()` uses epilog from helper function `_fixer_help_lines()` 

**USAGE_EXAMPLES Structure (lines 30-70):**
```
workflow:
  [list of high-level commands with descriptions]

examples:
  [actual CLI invocations]
```

#### 5. **Complete Argument Inventory by Command**

### 6. **Key Patterns & Conventions**

**Help Text Patterns:**
- All commands have minimal help strings (1-2 sentences)
- Long explanations go in epilogs (using `RawDescriptionHelpFormatter`)
- Attestation arguments have very detailed multi-line help (e.g., lines 236-240 in resolve)

**Deprecation Pattern:**
- Uses custom `_DeprecatedAction` and `_DeprecatedBoolAction` classes (lines 11-36)
- These print stderr warnings and store values
- Examples: `--max-age`, `--max-files`, `--refresh`, `--holistic` in review

**Metavar Usage:**
- Common: `FILE`, `PATTERN`, `DIR`, `KEY=VALUE`, `EXT`, `N`, `NAME`
- Applied consistently (e.g., all path outputs use `FILE` metavar)

**Type Coercion:**
- All use explicit `type=str|int|float`
- `action="store_true"` for boolean flags
- `choices=[...]` for enums
- `default=...` for defaults
- `nargs=...` for variadic args ("+", "?", 0)

**Nested Subcommands:**
- Properly nested: `plan` → `cluster` → create/add/remove/delete/move/show/list
- Uses `dest=...` to name the subcommand capture variable
- Required subcommands use `required=True` (line 585 in dev)

### 7. **What's Missing (Help Text Gaps)**

The following commands/arguments lack help text (empty or minimal):
- `scan --path` (no help)
- `scan --state` (no help)
- `status --state` (no help)
- `status --json` (no help)
- `tree --path` (no help)
- `tree --state` (no help)
- `tree --focus` (no help)
- `show --state` (no help)
- `next --state` (no help)
- `next --tier` (no help, but has choices)
- `resolve --note` (no help)
- `detect --top` (no help)
- `detect --path` (no help)
- `detect --json` (no help)
- `detect --file` (no help)
- `fix --path` (no help)
- `fix --state` (no help)
- `plan --state` (no help)
- `plan --output` (no help)
- `viz --path` (no help)
- `viz --output` (no help)
- `viz --state` (no help)
- `move --source` (partial help)
- `move --dest` (partial help)
- `review --path` (no help)
- `review --state` (no help)
- `zone --path` (no help)
- `zone --state` (no help)

### Summary

The parser infrastructure is **well-organized but minimally documented**. No argument groups are used; instead, simple sequential `add_argument()` calls build a flat list. Epilogs contain multi-line examples and formatter-aware text. The system uses custom deprecation actions and proper nesting for complex command families. **Most `--state`, `--path`, and `--output` arguments lack help text**, which would be good targets for improvement.

## Summary: Current Plan/Queue Output & Available Data

I've thoroughly examined the rendering logic across all plan-related commands. Here's what's currently available:

### **Command Output Overview**

#### **`desloppify plan` (no subcommand) — generates prioritized markdown**
**Output**: Full markdown plan document with:
- Header: overall/objective/strict scores, file/LOC/directory counts

  - Each item shows: tier/confidence badge, summary, id, description, note
- **Remaining section** (items NOT in plan): all other mechanical items by tier/file
- **Skipped section** (if plan exists): grouped by kind (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
- **Superseded section** (if plan exists): broken plan refs
- **Addressed section**: fixed/auto-resolved/wontfix counts + wontfix explanations

**Key limitation**: No compact queue overview. It's either full markdown or nothing.

---

#### **`desloppify plan show` — plan metadata summary**
**Output**: Text summary of living plan state:
```
  Ordered items:    N
  Skipped:          N (temp: X, wontfix: Y, fp: Z)
  Clusters:         N
    cluster_name: M items — description (focused)
  Overrides:        N
  Focus:            cluster_name
  Superseded:       N
```

**Insight**: Shows plan structure metadata but NO queue preview or ordering info.

---

#### **`desloppify next` — next highest-priority item(s)**
**Output** (terminal):
- Tier Navigator header: T1/T2/T3/T4 counts with tier-switch commands
- Optionally "Focused on: cluster_name (N of M remaining)"
- Single top item OR grouped items with details:
  - Tier/confidence badge, summary, file, id
  - Plan metadata if present: description, cluster membership, note, skip status
  - Dimension context, score impact estimate
  - Resolution hints and auto-fix suggestions
- Followup nudges: dimension gaps, subjective status

**Output** (JSON/query.json):
```json
{
  "command": "next",
  "items": [...],
  "queue": {
    "tier_counts": {1: N, 2: N, 3: N, 4: N},
    "requested_tier": ...,
    "selected_tier": ...,
    "total": N
  },
  "plan": {
    "active": true,
    "focus": "cluster_name",
    "clusters": [{name, description, item_count}, ...],
    "total_ordered": N,
    "total_skipped": N
  }
}
```

---

#### **`desloppify status` — score dashboard**
**Output**: 
- Score summary (overall/objective/strict/verified)
- Scan metrics, completeness, open scope breakdown
- Dimension health table with progress bars
- Focus suggestion (lowest dimension or active cluster)
- Subjective followup nudges
- Review summary
- Structural debt by area (T3/T4, grouped by area)
- **Agent Plan header** (if plan active):
  - Shows focus cluster + remaining count
  - Otherwise shows top 1-2 narrative actions
- Ignore list + suppression metrics

**Output** (JSON/query.json):
```json
{
  "command": "status",
  "overall_score": ...,
  "dimension_scores": {...},
  "plan": {
    "active": true,
    "focus": "cluster_name",
    "total_ordered": N,
    "total_skipped": N
  }
}
```

---

### **What Data is Available for Queue Discovery?**

From the code, the living plan has this full structure (`.desloppify/plan.json`):

```python
{
  "queue_order": [fid1, fid2, ...],      # User-ordered item IDs
  "clusters": {
    "cluster_name": {
      "finding_ids": [fid, ...],         # Cluster membership
      "description": "...",              # User description
      "action": "desloppify fix ...",    # Cluster action command
      "auto": True/False
    },
    ...
  },
  "skipped": {
    fid: {
      "kind": "temporary|permanent|false_positive",
      "reason": "...",
      "note": "...",
      "skipped_at_scan": N,
      "review_after": N
    },
    ...
  },
  "overrides": {
    fid: {
      "description": "...",             # User override description
      "note": "...",                    # User override note
      "cluster": "cluster_name"
    },
    ...
  },
  "active_cluster": "cluster_name",     # Focused cluster (or None)
  "superseded": {
    fid: {
      "original_summary": "...",
      "candidates": [alt_fid, ...],
      "note": "..."
    },
    ...
  }
}
```

Each work queue item also carries plan metadata when rendering:
- `queue_position` — position in merged queue
- `plan_description`, `plan_note` — overrides
- `plan_cluster` — cluster membership with name/desc/total_items
- `plan_skipped`, `plan_skip_kind`, `plan_skip_reason` — skip status

---

### **Key Findings for Discoverability**

1. **No compact queue view currently exists** — `plan show` is metadata-only; `plan` (no subcommand) is full markdown; `next` shows 1 item by default

2. **Available for a compact queue overview**:
   - `tier_counts` from work queue (T1/T2/T3/T4 open counts)
   - `queue_order` length (total ordered items)
   - Number of clusters and their sizes
   - Number of skipped items by kind
   - Active cluster name + remaining member count
   - Superseded count

3. **Item-level data available per queue position**:
   - Tier, confidence, detector, file, summary, id
   - Plan description, note, cluster membership
   - Skip status + reason
   - Dimension impact (if dimension_scores exist)
   - Auto-fix capability

4. **What's NOT currently surfaced**:
   - A **quick-reference queue table** showing N items in order with tier/status/cluster assignment
   - **Progress bars** for ordered vs remaining vs skipped
   - **Cluster membership visualization** (which items are in which cluster)
   - **Queue position numbers** in terminal output (only in JSON)

---

### **Recommendation**

A new `desloppify plan view` subcommand (or `desloppify queue`) could offer:
- **Compact table** of next 10-20 items with: `#Pos | Tier | Cluster | Summary | Status`
- **Stats bar** showing: `5 ordered | 12 remaining | 3 skipped | 2 clusters`
- **Tier breakdown** showing distribution across T1/T2/T3/T4
- **Cluster expansion** to drill into members

All data already exists in the work queue + plan; just needs a compact rendering layer.

I need to design improvements to the desloppify CLI's discoverability. Two interconnected goals:

## Goal 1: Richer --help output

The current state:
- 18 top-level commands in a flat list with terse one-liners
- `plan` has 11 subcommands + 7 nested cluster subcommands — no conceptual explanation
- `review` has 40+ options in a flat wall
- Many arguments lack help text entirely (--path, --state, --output, --json on many commands)
- No workflow guidance — users don't know when to use what
- No examples on individual command help pages (only on top-level)

File to modify: `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` (all parser builder functions)
Also: `desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py` (top-level parser, USAGE_EXAMPLES)

### Specific improvements needed:

1. **Top-level `--help`**: Better command grouping. Currently there's a "workflow:" section in the epilog that re-lists commands. Could use argparse subparser groups or improve the epilog to clearly show: Core Workflow (scan → status → next → resolve), Investigation (show, tree, detect), Plan Management (plan), and Admin/Setup (zone, config, dev, langs, etc.)

2. **Missing help text**: Add help= to all args that lack it. Common ones used across many commands:
   - `--path`: "Project root directory (default: cwd)"
   - `--state`: "Path to state file (default: .desloppify/state-{lang}.json)"
   - `--output FILE`: "Write output to file instead of terminal"
   - `--json`: "Output as JSON"

3. **Plan command**: Needs a description/epilog explaining the concept, workflow, and examples:
   - What the "living plan" is
   - Typical workflow: scan → plan → focus on cluster → next → resolve
   - Examples for common operations

4. **Review command**: Group the 40+ options using `add_argument_group()`:
   - "Core options" (--prepare, --import, --validate-import, --allow-partial)
   - "External review" (--external-start, --external-submit, --session-id, --external-runner, --session-ttl-hours, --attested-external)
   - "Batch execution" (--run-batches, --runner, --parallel, --max-parallel-batches, --batch-timeout-seconds, etc.)
   - "Trust & attestation" (--manual-override, --attest, --attested-external)
   - "Advanced" (--merge, --similarity, --packet, --only-batches, etc.)

5. **Individual command epilogs**: Add brief examples to key commands (show, resolve, next, plan subcommands)

## Goal 2: Compact queue overview

Currently there's no way to see a quick overview of the full upcoming work queue:
- `plan` (no subcommand) generates full verbose markdown
- `plan show` only shows metadata counts
- `next` shows 1 item by default
- `next --count N` shows N items but no structural overview

The user wants a stripped-back view showing all upcoming items compactly so they can see the full picture within limited context.

**Option A**: Enhance `plan show` to include a compact queue table
**Option B**: Add `plan queue` subcommand for a table view
**Option C**: Enhance `next --count all --format compact` or similar

All the data exists in the work queue (tier, cluster, summary, position) — just needs compact rendering.

### Constraints:
- Must work within argparse's capabilities
- Use `RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` which is already set up
- Don't break existing command behavior
- Keep help text concise — walls of text defeat the purpose
- The custom `_NoAbbrevArgumentParser` class is already used

### Questions to answer:
1. What's the best approach for grouping top-level commands?
2. Which specific commands most need epilog examples?
3. Where should the compact queue view live?
4. Should we use argparse argument groups or just better epilog organization for review?

Design a concrete implementation plan with specific text changes and file locations.

```
    33→  status                        Score dashboard with per-tier progress
    34→  tree                          Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    35→  show <pattern>                Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID

    58→  desloppify resolve fixed "logs::src/foo.tsx::*" --note "removed debug logs" --attest "I have actually removed all console.log calls from foo.tsx and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    59→  desloppify resolve wontfix deprecated --note "migration in progress" --attest "I have actually verified these are tracked in the migration plan and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    60→  desloppify ignore "smells::*::async_no_await" --attest "I have actually verified these are intentional fire-and-forget patterns and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    61→  desloppify detect logs --top 10

    64→  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude

    66→  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import --retrospective
    67→  desloppify dev scaffold-lang go --extension .go --marker go.mod --default-src .

    69→  desloppify move scripts/foo/bar.py scripts/foo/baz/bar.py
    70→"""
    71→
    72→
    73→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    74→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    75→
    76→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    77→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    78→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    82→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    83→    try:
    84→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    85→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    86→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    87→
    88→
    89→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    90→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    91→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    92→
    93→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    94→        prog="desloppify",
    95→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    96→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    97→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    98→    )
    99→    parser.add_argument(
   100→        "--lang",
   101→        type=str,
   102→        default=None,
   103→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   104→    )
   105→    parser.add_argument(
   106→        "--exclude",
   107→        action="append",
   108→        default=None,
   109→        metavar="PATTERN",
   110→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   111→    )
   112→    parser.add_argument(
   113→        "--version",
   114→        "-V",
   115→        action="version",
   116→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   117→    )
   118→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   119→        dest="command",
   120→        required=True,
   121→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   122→    )
   123→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   127→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_resolve_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   130→    _add_exclude_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   132→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   135→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   137→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   138→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   139→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   140→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   141→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   142→    return parser
   143→
   144→
   145→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   146→
```

```
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)

    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)

    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)

   394→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   395→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   396→    for lang_name in langs:
   397→        try:
   398→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   399→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   400→            fixer_names = []
   401→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   402→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   403→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   404→    return fixer_help_lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   408→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   409→        "fix",

   411→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   412→    )
   413→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   414→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None)
   415→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None)
   416→    p_fix.add_argument(
   417→        "--dry-run",
   418→        action="store_true",
   419→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   420→    )
   421→
```

```
1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    33→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    34→    print(f"  Ordered items:    {ordered}")
    35→    if total_skipped:
    36→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    37→    else:
    38→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    39→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    40→    if clusters:
    41→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    42→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    43→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    44→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    45→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    46→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    47→    print(f"  Overrides:        {len(overrides)}")
    48→    if active:
    49→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    50→    if superseded:
    51→        print(f"  Superseded:       {superseded}")
    52→
    53→
    54→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    56→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    57→
    58→    plan = load_plan()
    59→    reset_plan(plan)
    60→    save_plan(plan)
    61→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    62→
    63→
    64→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    65→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    66→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    67→
    68→    if plan_action is None:
    69→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    if plan_action == "show":
    73→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if plan_action == "reset":
    77→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if plan_action == "move":
    81→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
    82→
    83→        cmd_plan_move(args)
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if plan_action in (
    87→        "describe", "note", "focus",
    88→        "skip", "unskip", "done", "reopen",
    89→    ):
    90→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
    91→            cmd_plan_describe,
    92→            cmd_plan_done,
    93→            cmd_plan_focus,
    94→            cmd_plan_note,
    95→            cmd_plan_reopen,
    96→            cmd_plan_skip,
    97→            cmd_plan_unskip,
    98→        )
    99→
   100→        dispatch = {
   101→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   102→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   103→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   104→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   105→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   106→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   107→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   108→        }
   109→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   113→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   114→
   115→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   119→
   120→
   121→# Backwards-compatible alias
   122→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   123→
   124→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   125→
```

```
1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import render as next_render_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    15→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    16→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
    19→    QueueBuildOptions,
    20→    build_work_queue,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    23→from desloppify.core.skill_docs import check_skill_version
    24→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_tool_staleness
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    26→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    27→
    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    if config.get("needs_rescan"):
    76→        print(colorize("  Config changed — scores may be stale. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    77→
    78→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    82→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    83→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    84→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    85→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    86→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    87→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    88→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    89→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    90→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    91→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    92→
    93→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    94→
    95→    # Load the living plan
    96→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    97→    plan = load_plan()
    98→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    99→    if (
   100→        plan.get("queue_order")
   101→        or plan.get("overrides")
   102→        or plan.get("clusters")
   103→    ):
   104→        plan_data = plan
   105→
   106→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   107→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   108→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   109→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   110→        if active_cluster:
   111→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   112→
   113→    queue = build_work_queue(
   114→        state,
   115→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   116→            tier=tier,
   117→            count=count,
   118→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   119→            scope=scope,
   120→            status=status,
   121→            include_subjective=True,
   122→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   123→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   124→            explain=explain,
   125→            plan=plan_data,
   126→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   127→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   128→        ),
   129→    )
   130→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   131→
   132→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   133→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   134→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   135→        state,
   136→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   140→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   141→    )
   142→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   143→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   144→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   145→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   146→        state,
   147→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   148→    )
   149→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   150→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   151→    ]
   152→    write_query(payload)
   153→
   154→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   155→    if output_file:
   156→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   157→            output_file,
   158→            payload,
   159→            len(items),
   160→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   161→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   162→        ):
   163→            return
   164→        raise SystemExit(1)
   165→
   166→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   167→        return
   168→
   169→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   170→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   171→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   175→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   176→        state.get("findings", {}),
   177→        state.get("scan_path"),
   178→    )
   179→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   180→    try:
   181→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   182→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   183→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   184→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   185→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   186→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   187→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   188→    )
   189→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   190→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   191→        state,
   192→        dim_scores,
   193→        findings_scoped,
   194→        strict_score=strict_score,
   195→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   196→    )
   197→    print()
   198→
   199→
   200→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   201→
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

```
   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   339→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   340→        return
   341→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   342→    if not explain:
   343→        return
   344→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   345→    if available:
   346→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   348→
   349→
   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   355→    if tier is not None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(
   360→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True

   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,
   369→    findings_scoped: dict,
   370→    *,
   371→    group: str,
   372→    explain: bool,
   373→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   374→    plan: dict | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→
   401→
   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   431→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   432→    )
   433→    print(
   434→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = [
   506→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   507→    "render_followup_nudges",
   508→    "render_queue_header",
   509→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   510→    "render_terminal_items",
   511→    "scorecard_subjective",
   512→    "show_empty_queue",
   513→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   514→]
   515→
```
