1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    43→
    44→
    45→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    46→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    47→
    48→    items: list[dict]
    49→    total: int
    50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    51→    requested_tier: int | None
    52→    selected_tier: int | None
    53→    fallback_reason: str | None
    54→    available_tiers: list[int]
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
   145→
   146→
   147→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   149→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   150→        return any(
   151→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   152→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   153→        )
   154→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   155→
   156→
   157→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   158→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   159→    try:
   160→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   161→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   162→        if meta:
   163→            return meta.action_type
   164→    except ImportError:
   165→        pass
   166→    return "manual_fix"
   167→
   168→
   169→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   170→
   171→
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   174→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   175→    if not clusters:
   176→        return items
   177→
   178→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   179→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   180→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   181→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   182→            continue
   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   184→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   185→
   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   187→        return items
   188→
   189→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   190→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   191→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   192→
   193→    for item in items:
   194→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   195→        if cname:
   196→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   199→
   200→    # Build cluster meta-items
   201→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   202→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   203→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   204→        if len(members) < 2:
   205→            result.extend(members)
   206→            continue
   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   208→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   209→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   210→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   211→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   212→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   213→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   214→            action_type = "reorganize"
   215→        else:
   216→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   217→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   218→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   219→                action_type = "refactor"
   220→
   221→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   222→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   223→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   224→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   225→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   226→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   227→        else:
   228→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   229→
   230→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   231→        if not primary_command:
   232→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   233→
   234→        meta_item: dict = {
   235→            "id": cname,
   236→            "kind": "cluster",
   237→            "action_type": action_type,
   238→            "summary": summary,
   239→            "members": members,
   240→            "member_count": len(members),
   241→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   242→            "cluster_name": cname,
   243→            "cluster_auto": True,
   244→            "confidence": "high",
   245→            "detector": detector,
   246→            "file": "",
   247→        }
   248→        result.append(meta_item)
   249→
   250→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   251→    return result
   252→
   253→
   254→def build_work_queue(
   255→    state: StateModel,
   256→    *,
   257→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   258→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   259→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   260→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   261→
   262→    status = resolved_options.status
   263→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   264→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   265→    try:
   266→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   267→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   268→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   269→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   270→
   271→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   272→        state,
   273→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   274→        status_filter=status,
   275→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   276→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   277→    )
   278→
   279→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   280→    if (
   281→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   282→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   283→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   284→    ):
   285→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   286→            state,
   287→            state.get("findings", {}),
   288→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   289→        )
   290→        for item in subjective_items:
   291→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   292→                all_items.append(item)
   293→
   294→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   295→
   296→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   297→    if resolved_options.plan:
   298→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   299→            all_items,
   300→            resolved_options.plan,
   301→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   302→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   303→        )
   304→
   305→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   306→    should_collapse = (
   307→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   308→        and resolved_options.plan
   309→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   310→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   311→    )
   312→    if should_collapse:
   313→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   314→
   315→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   316→
   317→    requested_tier = (
   318→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   319→    )
   320→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   321→    fallback_reason = None
   322→    filtered = all_items
   323→
   324→    if requested_tier is not None:
   325→        filtered = [
   326→            item
   327→            for item in all_items
   328→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   329→        ]
   330→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   331→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   332→            if chosen is not None:
   333→                selected_tier = chosen
   334→                filtered = [
   335→                    item
   336→                    for item in all_items
   337→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   338→                ]
   339→                fallback_reason = (
   340→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   341→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   342→                )
   343→        elif not filtered:
   344→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   345→
   346→    total = len(filtered)
   347→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   348→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   349→
   350→    if resolved_options.explain:
   351→        for item in filtered:
   352→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   353→
   354→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   355→    return {
   356→        "items": filtered,
   357→        "total": total,
   358→        "tier_counts": counts,
   359→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   360→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   361→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   362→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   363→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   364→    }
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   369→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   370→    "WorkQueueResult",
   371→    "build_work_queue",
   372→    "group_queue_items",
   373→]
   374→

1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from datetime import date
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import TIER_LABELS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES

    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD

    16→
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (
    24→        overall_score is not None
    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def _plan_user_ordered_section(
   292→    items: list[dict],
   293→    plan: dict,
   294→) -> list[str]:
   295→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
   296→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   297→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   298→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   299→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   300→
   301→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
   302→    if not ordered_ids:
   303→        return []
   304→
   305→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   306→
   307→    lines: list[str] = [
   308→        "---",
   309→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
   310→        "",
   311→    ]
   312→
   313→    # Group by cluster: clustered items first, then unclustered
   314→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   315→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   316→        member_ids = [
   317→            fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   318→            if fid in ordered_ids and fid in by_id
   319→        ]
   320→        if not member_ids:
   321→            continue
   322→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   323→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   324→        if desc:
   325→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   326→        lines.append("")
   327→        for fid in member_ids:
   328→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   329→            if item:
   330→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   331→                emitted.add(fid)
   332→        lines.append("")
   333→
   334→    # Unclustered ordered items
   335→    unclustered = [
   336→        fid for fid in queue_order
   337→        if fid in ordered_ids and fid not in emitted and fid in by_id
   338→    ]
   339→    if unclustered:
   340→        if any(c.get("finding_ids") for c in clusters.values()):
   341→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   342→            lines.append("")
   343→        for fid in unclustered:
   344→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   345→            if item:
   346→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   347→        lines.append("")
   348→
   349→    return lines
   350→
   351→
   352→def _plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   353→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   354→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   355→    if not skipped:
   356→        return []
   357→
   358→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   359→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   360→
   361→    # Group by kind
   362→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   363→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   364→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   365→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(fid)
   366→
   367→    kind_labels = {
   368→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   369→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   370→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    lines: list[str] = [
   374→        "---",
   375→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   376→        "",
   377→    ]
   378→
   379→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   380→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   381→        if not ids:
   382→            continue
   383→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   384→        lines.append("")
   385→        for fid in ids:
   386→            entry = skipped.get(fid, {})
   387→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   388→            if item:
   389→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   390→            else:
   391→                lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~ (not in current queue)")
   392→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   393→            if reason:
   394→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   395→            note = entry.get("note")
   396→            if note and not overrides.get(fid, {}).get("note"):
   397→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   398→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   399→            if review_after:
   400→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   401→                lines.append(f"      Review after: scan {skipped_at + review_after}")
   402→        lines.append("")
   403→
   404→    return lines
   405→
   406→
   407→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   409→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   410→    if not superseded:
   411→        return []
   412→
   413→    lines: list[str] = [
   414→        "---",
   415→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   416→        "",
   417→    ]
   418→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   419→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   420→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   421→        lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~{summary_str}")
   422→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   423→        if candidates:
   424→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   425→        note = entry.get("note")
   426→        if note:
   427→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   428→    lines.append("")
   429→    return lines
   430→
   431→
   432→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   433→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   434→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   435→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   436→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   437→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   438→
   439→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   440→    desc = override.get("description")
   441→    if desc:
   442→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   443→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   444→    note = override.get("note")
   445→    if note:
   446→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   447→    return lines
   448→
   449→
   450→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   451→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   452→
   453→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   454→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   455→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   456→    """
   457→    findings = state["findings"]
   458→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   459→
   460→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   461→    if plan is None:
   462→        try:
   463→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   464→            plan = load_plan()
   465→        except Exception:
   466→            plan = {}
   467→
   468→    has_plan = bool(
   469→        plan
   470→        and (
   471→            plan.get("queue_order")
   472→            or plan.get("skipped")
   473→            or plan.get("clusters")
   474→        )
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   478→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   479→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   480→
   481→    if has_plan:
   482→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   483→        queue = build_work_queue(
   484→            state,
   485→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   486→                count=None,
   487→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   488→                status="open",
   489→                include_subjective=True,
   490→                no_tier_fallback=True,
   491→            ),
   492→        )
   493→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   494→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   495→
   496→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   497→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   498→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   499→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   500→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   501→        if remaining:
   502→            lines.append("---")
   503→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   504→            lines.append("")
   505→
   506→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   507→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   508→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   509→    else:
   510→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   511→
   512→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   513→
   514→    return "\n".join(lines)
   515→
```

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,

    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    88→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    89→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    90→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    91→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    92→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    93→        )
    94→        return (
    95→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    96→            action_pri,
    97→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    98→            item.get("id", ""),
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   102→        return (
   103→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   104→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   105→            subjective_score_value(item),
   106→            item.get("id", ""),
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   110→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   111→    return (
   112→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   113→        0,
   114→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   115→        -review_weight,
   116→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   117→        item.get("id", ""),
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   122→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   123→
   124→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   125→        return {
   126→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   127→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   128→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   129→            "policy": (
   130→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   131→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   132→            ),
   133→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   134→        }
   135→
   136→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   137→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   138→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   139→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   140→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   141→    if is_subjective:
   142→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   143→    elif is_review:
   144→        ranking_factors = [
   145→            "tier asc",
   146→            "confidence asc",
   147→            "review_weight desc",
   148→            "count desc",
   149→            "id asc",
   150→        ]
   151→    else:
   152→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   153→    explain = {
   154→        "kind": "finding",
   155→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   156→        "confidence": confidence,
   157→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   158→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   159→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   160→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   161→    }
   162→    if is_review:
   163→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   164→    if is_subjective:
   165→        explain["policy"] = (
   166→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   167→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   168→        )
   169→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   170→    return explain
   171→
   172→
   173→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   174→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   175→    for item in items:
   176→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   177→            # Count each member at its own tier
   178→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   179→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   180→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   181→            continue
   182→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   183→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   184→    return counts
   185→
   186→
   187→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   188→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   189→    if not available:
   190→        return None
   191→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   192→
   193→
   194→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   195→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   196→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   197→    for item in items:
   198→        if group == "file":
   199→            key = item.get("file", "")
   200→        elif group == "detector":
   201→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   202→        elif group == "tier":
   203→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   204→        elif group == "cluster":
   205→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   206→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   207→        else:
   208→            key = "items"
   209→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   210→    return grouped
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "build_finding_items",
   215→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "tier_counts",
   219→    "subjective_score_value",
   220→    "group_queue_items",
   221→]
   222→
```

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    41→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    42→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    43→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    44→
    45→
    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    68→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    69→
    70→
    71→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    72→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    73→    now = utc_now()
    74→    return {
    75→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    76→        "created": now,
    77→        "updated": now,
    78→        "queue_order": [],
    79→        "deferred": [],
    80→        "skipped": {},
    81→        "active_cluster": None,
    82→        "overrides": {},
    83→        "clusters": {},
    84→        "superseded": {},
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    89→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    90→
    91→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    92→    """
    93→    defaults = empty_plan()
    94→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    95→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    96→
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    98→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   100→        plan["deferred"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   102→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   104→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   105→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   106→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   108→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   109→
   110→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   111→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   112→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   113→    if deferred:
   114→        now = utc_now()
   115→        for fid in list(deferred):
   116→            if fid not in skipped:
   117→                skipped[fid] = {
   118→                    "finding_id": fid,
   119→                    "kind": "temporary",
   120→                    "reason": None,
   121→                    "note": None,
   122→                    "attestation": None,
   123→                    "created_at": now,
   124→                    "review_after": None,
   125→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   126→                }
   127→        deferred.clear()
   128→
   129→    # Normalize cluster fields
   130→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   131→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   132→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   133→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   134→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   135→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   136→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   137→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   138→
   139→
   140→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   141→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   142→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   143→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   144→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   145→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   146→
   147→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   148→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   149→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   150→    if overlap:
   151→        raise ValueError(
   152→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   153→        )
   154→
   155→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   156→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   157→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   158→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   159→            raise ValueError(
   160→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   161→            )
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   166→    "Cluster",
   167→    "ItemOverride",
   168→    "PlanModel",
   169→    "SkipEntry",
   170→    "SupersededEntry",
   171→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   172→    "empty_plan",
   173→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   174→    "validate_plan",
   175→]
   176→

    42→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    66→            )
    67→        )
    68→        return

    71→        return

    77→    if skill_warning:
    78→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    79→    if config.get("needs_rescan"):

    81→
    82→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    83→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})

    34→
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:
    36→    """Fallback display when dimension scores are unavailable."""
    37→    rows = []
    38→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
    39→        ts = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
    40→        t_open = ts.get("open", 0)
    41→        t_fixed = ts.get("fixed", 0) + ts.get("auto_resolved", 0)
    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)
    43→        t_wontfix = ts.get("wontfix", 0)
    44→        t_total = sum(ts.values())
    45→        strict_pct = round((t_fixed + t_fp) / t_total * 100) if t_total else 100
    46→        bar_len = 20
    47→        filled = round(strict_pct / 100 * bar_len)
    48→        bar = colorize("█" * filled, "green") + colorize(
    49→            "░" * (bar_len - filled), "dim"
    50→        )
    51→        rows.append(
    52→            [
    53→                f"Tier {tier_num}",
    54→                bar,
    55→                f"{strict_pct}%",
    56→                str(t_open),
    57→                str(t_fixed),
    58→                str(t_wontfix),
    59→            ]
    60→        )
    61→    print_table(
    62→        ["Tier", "Strict Progress", "%", "Open", "Fixed", "Debt"],
    63→        rows,
    64→        [40, 22, 5, 6, 6, 6],
    65→    )
    66→

    68→def _status_next_command(narrative: dict) -> str:
    69→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
    70→    return actions[0]["command"] if actions else "desloppify next --count 20"
    71→
    72→
    73→def write_status_query(
    74→    *,
    75→    state: dict,
    76→    stats: dict,
    77→    by_tier: dict,
    78→    dim_scores: dict,
    79→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    80→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    81→    suppression: dict,
    82→    narrative: dict,
    83→    ignores: list[str],
    84→    overall_score: float | None,
    85→    objective_score: float | None,
    86→    strict_score: float | None,
    87→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    88→    plan: dict | None = None,
    89→) -> None:
    90→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    91→    open_scope = (
    92→        state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    93→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    94→        else None
    95→    )
    96→    write_query(
    97→        {
    98→            "command": "status",

   131→    print(colorize(f"\n  Ignore list ({len(ignores)}):", "dim"))

   151→    recent_raw = int(suppression.get("recent_raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   152→    if recent_scans > 1 and recent_raw > 0:
   153→        recent_ignored = int(suppression.get("recent_ignored", 0) or 0)
   154→        recent_pct = float(suppression.get("recent_suppressed_pct", 0.0) or 0.0)

   163→def _scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   164→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
   165→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
   166→        state,

   170→
   171→def _find_lowest_dimension(
   172→    dim_scores: dict,
   173→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   174→) -> str | None:
   175→    """Return the name of the dimension with the lowest strict score."""
   176→    lowest_name = None
   177→    lowest_score = 101.0
   178→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   179→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   180→        if not ds:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   183→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   184→            lowest_score = strict_val
   185→            lowest_name = dim.name
   186→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   187→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   188→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
   189→            lowest_score = strict_val
   190→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
   191→    return lowest_name
   192→
   193→
   194→def _open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
   195→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
   196→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   197→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
   198→        return {}
   199→
   200→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
   201→    for finding in findings.values():
   202→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   203→            continue
   204→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   205→            continue
   206→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
   207→        dimension = ""
   208→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
   209→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   210→        if not dimension:
   211→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
   212→        if not dimension:
   213→            continue
   214→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
   215→    return counts
   216→
   217→
   218→def _render_objective_dimensions(
   219→    dim_scores: dict, *, lowest_name: str | None, bar_len: int
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
   222→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   223→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   224→        if not ds:
   225→            continue
   226→        score_val = ds["score"]
   227→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   228→        checks = ds["checks"]
   229→
   230→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   231→
   232→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
   233→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
   234→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   235→        print(
   236→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
   237→        )
   238→
   239→
   240→def _render_subjective_dimensions(
   241→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
   242→    *,
   243→    lowest_name: str | None,
   244→    bar_len: int,
   245→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   246→) -> None:
   247→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   248→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   249→        return
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   253→            "dim",
   254→        )
   255→    )
   256→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   257→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   258→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   259→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   260→        tier = 4
   261→
   262→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   263→
   264→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   265→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   266→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   267→        placeholder_tag = (
   268→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   269→        )
   270→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   271→        cli_keys = [
   272→            str(key).strip()
   273→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   274→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   275→        ]
   276→        if dim_key:
   277→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   278→        elif cli_keys:
   279→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   280→        else:
   281→            issue_count = 0
   282→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   283→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   284→        print(
   285→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   286→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   287→        )
   288→

   291→    """Print the legend footer and stale-dimension re-review hint."""
   292→    print(

   294→    )

   327→    print()

   352→def show_focus_suggestion(
   353→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   354→) -> None:
   355→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   356→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   357→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   358→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   359→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   360→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   361→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   362→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   363→        print(
   364→            colorize(
   365→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   366→                "cyan",
   367→            )
   368→        )
   369→        print()
   370→        return
   371→
   372→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   373→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   374→    if not lowest_name:
   375→        return
   376→
   377→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   378→    lowest_kind = None
   379→    lowest_score = 101.0
   380→    lowest_issues = 0
   381→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   382→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   383→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   384→            if ds:
   385→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   386→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   387→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   388→            break
   389→    else:
   390→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   391→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   392→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   393→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   394→                lowest_issues = 0
   395→                break
   396→
   397→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   398→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   399→            print(
   400→                colorize(
   401→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   402→                    "cyan",
   403→                )
   404→            )
   405→            print()
   406→            return
   407→
   408→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   409→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   410→        if target_dim:
   411→            impact = 0.0
   412→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   413→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   414→                    {
   415→                        k: {
   416→                            "score": v["score"],
   417→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   418→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   419→                        }
   420→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   421→                        if "score" in v
   422→                    },
   423→                    potentials,
   424→                    det,
   425→                    lowest_issues,
   426→                )
   427→                if impact > 0:
   428→                    break
   429→
   430→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   431→            print(
   432→                colorize(
   433→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   434→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   435→                    "cyan",
   436→                )
   437→            )
   438→            print()
   439→

   493→def _collect_structural_areas(
   494→    state: dict,
   495→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   496→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   497→
   498→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   499→    """
   500→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   501→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   502→    )
   503→
   504→    structural = [
   505→        f
   506→        for f in findings.values()
   507→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   508→    ]
   509→
   510→    if len(structural) < 5:
   511→        return None
   512→
   513→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   514→    for f in structural:
   515→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   516→        areas[area].append(f)
   517→
   518→    if len(areas) < 2:
   519→        return None
   520→
   521→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   522→
   523→
   524→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   525→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   526→    rows = []
   527→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   528→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   529→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   530→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   531→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   532→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   533→        rows.append(
   534→            [
   535→                area,
   536→                str(len(area_findings)),
   537→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   538→                str(open_count),
   539→                str(debt_count),
   540→                str(weight),
   541→            ]
   542→        )
   543→    return rows
   544→
   545→
   546→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   547→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   548→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   549→    if remaining > 0:
   550→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   551→
   552→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   553→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   554→    print(
   555→        colorize(
   556→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   557→        )
   558→    )
   559→    print(
   560→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   561→    )
   562→    print()
   563→
   564→
   565→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   566→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   567→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   568→    if sorted_areas is None:
   569→        return
   570→
   571→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   572→    print(
   573→        colorize(
   574→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   575→            "dim",
   576→        )
   577→    )
   578→    print()
   579→
   580→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   581→    print_table(
   582→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   583→    )
   584→
   585→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   586→

   588→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   589→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   590→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   591→    review_open = [
   592→        f
   593→        for f in findings.values()
   594→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   595→    ]
   596→    if not review_open:
   597→        return
   598→    uninvestigated = sum(
   599→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   600→    )
   601→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   602→    if uninvestigated:
   603→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   604→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   605→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   606→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   607→        print(
   608→            colorize(
   609→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   610→                "dim",
   611→            )
   612→        )
   613→    print()

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta

    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    12→

    18→
