```
    22→
    23→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    24→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    25→
    26→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    27→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    28→    """
    29→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    30→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    31→    if kind:
    32→        return kind
    33→
    34→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    35→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    36→    parts = fid.split("::")
    37→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    38→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    39→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    40→        candidate = parts[-1]
    41→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    42→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    43→            return candidate
    44→    return None
    45→
    46→
    47→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    48→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    49→
    50→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    51→    """
    52→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    53→
    54→    if meta is None:
    55→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    56→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    57→
    58→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    59→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    60→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    61→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    64→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    65→
    66→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    67→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    68→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    69→    ):
    70→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    71→        if file:
    72→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    73→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    74→
    75→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    76→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    77→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    78→        if subtype:
    79→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    80→
    81→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    82→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    84→
    85→    # Everything else → by detector
    86→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    87→
    88→
    89→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    90→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    91→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    92→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    93→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
    94→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
    95→    # detector::security → auto/security
    96→    parts = key.split("::")
    97→    if len(parts) == 2:
    98→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    99→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   100→        if prefix_type == "review":
   101→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   102→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   103→    if len(parts) == 3:
   104→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   105→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   106→
   107→
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→# Description and action generation
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→
   112→def _generate_description(
   113→    cluster_name: str,
   114→    members: list[dict],
   115→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   116→    subtype: str | None,
   117→) -> str:
   118→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   119→    count = len(members)
   120→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   121→
   122→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   123→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   124→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   125→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   126→
   127→    if detector == "structural":
   128→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   129→        if len(files) == 1:
   130→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   131→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   132→
   133→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   134→    if subtype:
   135→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   136→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   137→
   138→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   139→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   140→
   141→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   145→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   146→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   147→        return None
   148→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   149→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   150→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   151→        return fixer_name
   152→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   153→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   154→        if subtype in fixer:
   155→            return fixer
   156→    return None
   157→
   158→
   159→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   160→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   161→
   162→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   163→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   164→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   165→    """
   166→    if " — " in guidance:
   167→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   168→    return guidance
   169→
   170→
   171→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   172→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   173→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   174→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   175→}
   176→
   177→
   178→def _generate_action(
   179→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   180→    subtype: str | None,
   181→) -> str:
   182→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   183→
   184→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   185→    """
   186→    if meta is None:
   187→        return "review and fix each finding"
   188→
   189→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   190→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   191→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   192→        if matched_fixer:
   193→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   194→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   195→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   196→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   197→
   198→    if meta.tool == "move":
   199→        return "desloppify move"
   200→
   201→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   202→    if meta.guidance:
   203→        if subtype:
   204→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   205→        return meta.guidance
   206→
   207→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   208→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   209→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→# Main algorithm
   215→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216→
   217→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   218→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   219→
   220→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   221→    """
   222→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   223→    changes = 0
   224→
   225→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   226→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   227→
   228→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   229→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   230→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   231→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   232→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   233→
   234→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   235→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   236→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   237→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   238→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   239→            continue
   240→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   241→            continue
   242→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   243→            continue
   244→
   245→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   246→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   247→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   248→        if key is None:
   249→            continue
   250→
   251→        groups[key].append(fid)
   252→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   253→
   254→    # Drop singleton groups
   255→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   256→
   257→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   258→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   259→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   260→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   261→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   262→            if ck:
   263→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   264→
   265→    now = utc_now()
   266→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   267→
   268→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   269→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   270→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   271→
   272→        # Representative finding for metadata
   273→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   274→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   275→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   276→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   277→
   278→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   279→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   280→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   281→
   282→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   283→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   284→
   285→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   286→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   287→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   288→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   289→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   290→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   291→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   292→                if new_ids:
   293→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   294→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   295→                    changes += 1
   296→            else:
   297→                # Replace membership wholesale
   298→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   299→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   300→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   301→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   302→                    cluster["description"] = description
   303→                    cluster["action"] = action
   304→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   305→                    changes += 1
   306→        else:
   307→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   308→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   309→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   310→                # Append a disambiguator
   311→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   312→
   313→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   314→                "name": cluster_name,
   315→                "description": description,
   316→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   317→                "created_at": now,
   318→                "updated_at": now,
   319→                "auto": True,
   320→                "cluster_key": key,
   321→                "action": action,
   322→                "user_modified": False,
   323→            }
   324→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   325→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   326→            changes += 1
   327→
   328→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   329→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   330→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   331→        for fid in member_ids:
   332→            if fid not in overrides:
   333→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   334→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   335→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   336→
   337→    # --- Stale subjective dimension cluster --------------------------------
   338→    subjective_ids = sorted(
   339→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   340→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if len(subjective_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   344→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   345→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in subjective_ids]
   346→        description = f"Re-review {len(subjective_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   347→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   348→
   349→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   350→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   351→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   352→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   353→            new_ids = set(subjective_ids)
   354→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   355→                cluster["finding_ids"] = subjective_ids
   356→                cluster["description"] = description
   357→                cluster["action"] = action
   358→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   359→                changes += 1
   360→        else:
   361→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   362→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   363→                "description": description,
   364→                "finding_ids": subjective_ids,
   365→                "created_at": now,
   366→                "updated_at": now,
   367→                "auto": True,
   368→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   369→                "action": action,
   370→                "user_modified": False,
   371→            }
   372→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   373→            changes += 1
   374→
   375→        # Track cluster membership in overrides (same as main loop)
   376→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   377→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   378→        for fid in subjective_ids:
   379→            if fid not in overrides:
   380→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   381→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   382→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   383→
   384→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   385→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   386→        cluster = clusters[name]
   387→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   388→            continue
   389→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   390→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   391→            continue
   392→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   393→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   394→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   395→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   396→            if alive:
   397→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   398→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   399→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   400→                    changes += 1
   401→                continue
   402→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   403→        # Delete stale cluster
   404→        del clusters[name]
   405→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   406→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   407→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   408→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   409→                override["cluster"] = None
   410→                override["updated_at"] = now
   411→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   412→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   413→        changes += 1
   414→
   415→    plan["updated"] = now
   416→    return changes
   417→
   418→
   419→__all__ = [
   420→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   421→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   422→]
   423→
```

```
1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    39→    skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.", "dim"))
    43→    if active:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    45→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    47→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    48→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_replacement_groups(
    52→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    53→    *,
    54→    title: str,
    55→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    56→) -> None:
    57→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    58→    if not groups:
    59→        return
    60→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    61→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    62→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    63→        for old, new in replacements:
    64→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    65→    print()
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_ranked_actions(
    69→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    70→) -> bool:
    71→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    72→    ranked = sorted(
    73→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    74→        key=lambda action: (
    75→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    76→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    77→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    78→        ),
    79→    )
    80→    if not ranked:
    81→        return False
    82→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    83→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
    84→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
    85→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    86→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    87→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    88→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
    89→        if cluster_count:
    90→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    91→        else:
    92→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    93→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    94→    return True
    95→
    96→
    97→__all__ = [
    98→    "print_agent_plan",
    99→    "print_ranked_actions",
   100→    "print_replacement_groups",
   101→]
   102→
```

```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
    14→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    15→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    16→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    17→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path
    18→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    19→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    20→from desloppify.state import load_state
    21→
    22→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    23→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    24→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    25→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    26→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    27→
    28→
    29→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    30→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    31→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    32→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    33→        from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names
    34→
    35→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    36→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    37→
    38→def create_parser():
    39→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    40→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    41→
    42→
    43→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    44→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    45→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    46→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    47→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    48→    if not combined:
    49→        return
    50→    set_exclusions(combined)
    51→    if cli_exclusions:
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    54→            file=sys.stderr,
    55→        )
    56→        return
    57→    print(
    58→        colorize(
    59→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    60→        ),
    61→        file=sys.stderr,
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    66→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    67→
    68→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    69→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    70→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    71→    """
    72→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    73→        return
    74→    runtime_root = (
    75→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    76→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    77→        else get_project_root()
    78→    )
    79→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    80→        try:
    81→            state_file = state_path(args)
    82→            if state_file:
    83→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    84→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    85→                if saved_path:
    86→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    87→                    return
    88→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    89→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    90→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    91→    if lang:
    92→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
    93→    else:
    94→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
    95→
    96→
    97→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
    98→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
    99→    config = load_config()
   100→
   101→    state_file = state_path(args)
   102→    state = load_state(state_file)
   103→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   104→
   105→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   109→    from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
   110→
   111→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   112→
   113→
   114→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   115→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   116→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   117→    try:
   118→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   119→    except SystemExit:
   120→        return
   121→
   122→
   123→def main() -> None:
   124→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   125→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   126→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   127→            try:
   128→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   129→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   130→                logger.debug(
   131→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   132→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   133→                )
   134→
   135→    parser = create_parser()
   136→    args = parser.parse_args()
   137→    if args.command == "help":
   138→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    try:
   142→        with runtime_scope():
   143→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   144→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   145→
   146→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   147→            handler(args)
   148→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   149→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   150→        sys.exit(1)
   151→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   152→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   153→        sys.exit(1)
   154→
   155→
   156→if __name__ == "__main__":
   157→    main()
   158→
```

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the codebase. Let me compile my complete design plan.

---

## Design Plan: CLI Discoverability Improvements

I have thoroughly explored the relevant codebase. Here is my concrete implementation plan addressing both goals.

---

### Answering the Design Questions First

**Q1: What is the best approach for grouping top-level commands?**

Argparse does not natively support subparser "groups" the way it supports `add_argument_group()` for arguments. The standard approach is to use the epilog string with `RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` to present a structured layout. The current epilog (`USAGE_EXAMPLES`) already does this. The cleanest approach is to restructure the epilog into clear sections: a concise command group reference at the top, then examples below. This avoids hacking argparse internals or overriding `format_help()`.

However, there is a second option worth considering: replacing `add_subparsers(dest="command")` with multiple calls and titles. Argparse only allows one `add_subparsers()` call per parser, so this is not viable. The epilog-based approach is correct.

**Q2: Which specific commands most need epilog examples?**

Based on complexity and frequency of use: `plan` (the living plan concept needs explaining), `show` (flexible pattern matching is confusing), `resolve` (attestation requirements), `next` (many filtering options), and `review` (mode diversity). `scan` and `status` are simpler and get less value from examples.

**Q3: Where should the compact queue view live?**

I recommend **Option B: `plan queue`** as the primary location, with the table also folded into `plan show` as an optional `--full` flag. Rationale:
- `plan queue` is a natural home -- "show me the plan queue" is intuitive.
- Separate from `plan show` (which shows metadata) avoids overloading a simple summary command.
- It reuses the same `build_work_queue` infrastructure as `next` but with unlimited count and compact rendering.
- Adding `--format compact` to `next` is also viable as a complement but conflates "what is next" with "show me everything."

**Q4: Should we use argparse argument groups or better epilog organization for review?**

Use `add_argument_group()`. This is the correct argparse mechanism. Argument groups control how `--help` renders option sections. The current review parser dumps 40+ options in a single "options" block. Breaking them into named groups produces clear visual separation with no custom formatting code.

---

### Part 1: Richer --help Output

#### 1A. Restructure Top-Level Epilog (`parser.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py`

Replace the current `USAGE_EXAMPLES` string. The new structure:

```
USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
core workflow:
  scan            Run all detectors, update state, show diff
  status          Score dashboard with per-tier progress
  next            Show next highest-priority item to work on
  resolve         Mark finding(s) as fixed/wontfix/false_positive
  plan            Living plan: prioritize, cluster, skip, track

investigation:
  show <pattern>  Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
  tree            Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
  detect          Run a single detector directly (bypass state)

maintenance:
  fix             Auto-fix mechanical issues (unused imports, logs, etc.)
  move            Move file/dir and update all import references
  review          Prepare or import holistic subjective review
  ignore          Suppress findings matching a pattern
  exclude         Add path pattern to exclude from scanning

admin:
  zone            Show/set/clear zone classifications
  config          Show/set/unset project configuration
  viz             Generate interactive HTML treemap
  dev             Developer utilities (scaffold-lang)
  langs           List available language plugins
  update-skill    Install/update the desloppify skill document

examples:
  desloppify scan --skip-slow
  desloppify --lang python scan --path scripts/desloppify
  desloppify show src/shared/components/MediaLightbox
  desloppify show gods
  desloppify next --count 10 --group file
  desloppify resolve fixed "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" --note "removed" --attest "..."
  desloppify plan queue
  desloppify plan focus my-cluster
"""
```

Key changes:
- Commands grouped into 4 clear categories with headers
- Removed redundant help text that merely repeats the subcommand descriptions
- Trimmed examples to the most useful ones (removed very long lines)
- Added `plan queue` example (ties to Goal 2)

#### 1C. Plan Command Epilog and Description

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py`

Modify `_add_plan_parser` (line 423) to add a description and epilog:

```python
def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
        "plan",
        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
        description=(
            "Manage the living plan — a persistent, user-curated layer on top\n"
            "of the mechanical work queue. The plan tracks custom ordering,\n"
            "clusters (grouped findings), skips (temporary/permanent), and\n"
            "per-finding descriptions and notes."
        ),
        epilog=(
            "typical workflow:\n"
            "  1. desloppify scan                 # detect findings\n"
            "  2. desloppify plan                  # see full prioritized markdown\n"
            "  3. desloppify plan queue             # compact table of all items\n"
            "  4. desloppify plan focus my-cluster  # narrow scope to a cluster\n"
            "  5. desloppify next                   # work on the next item\n"
            "  6. desloppify plan done <id> --attest '...'  # mark as fixed\n"
            "\n"
            "subcommands:\n"
            "  (none)           Generate full prioritized markdown plan\n"
```

#### 1D. Review Command Argument Groups

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py`

Restructure `_add_review_parser` to use `add_argument_group()`. The key change is organizing the 40+ arguments into logical groups:

```python
def _add_review_parser(sub) -> None:
    p_review = sub.add_parser(
        "review",
        help="Prepare or import holistic subjective review",
        description=(
            "Manage holistic subjective reviews. Reviews assess dimensions like\n"
            "abstraction fitness, naming quality, and test health using LLM-based\n"
            "evaluation. Results feed into the strict score."
        ),
        epilog=(
            "examples:\n"
            "  desloppify review --prepare\n"
            "  desloppify review --import findings.json\n"
            "  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel\n"
            "  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude\n"
            "  desloppify review --external-submit --session-id <id> --import findings.json"
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    )

    # --- Core options ---
    core = p_review.add_argument_group("core options")
    core.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None,
                      help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
    core.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None,
                      help="Path to state file (default: .desloppify/state-{lang}.json)")
    core.add_argument("--prepare", action="store_true",
                      help="Prepare review data (output to query.json)")
    core.add_argument("--import", dest="import_file", ...)
    core.add_argument("--validate-import", ...)
    core.add_argument("--allow-partial", ...)
    core.add_argument("--dimensions", ...)
    core.add_argument("--retrospective", ...)
    core.add_argument("--retrospective-max-issues", ...)
    core.add_argument("--retrospective-max-batch-items", ...)
    core.add_argument("--force-review-rerun", ...)

    # --- External review ---
    external = p_review.add_argument_group("external review (cloud reviewer sessions)")
    external.add_argument("--external-start", ...)
    external.add_argument("--external-submit", ...)
    external.add_argument("--session-id", ...)
    external.add_argument("--external-runner", ...)
    external.add_argument("--session-ttl-hours", ...)

    # --- Batch execution ---
    batch = p_review.add_argument_group("batch execution (subagent runners)")
    batch.add_argument("--run-batches", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--runner", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--parallel", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--max-parallel-batches", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--batch-timeout-seconds", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--batch-max-retries", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--batch-retry-backoff-seconds", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--batch-heartbeat-seconds", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--batch-stall-warning-seconds", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--batch-stall-kill-seconds", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--dry-run", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--save-run-log", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--run-log-file", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--only-batches", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--scan-after-import", ...)
    batch.add_argument("--packet", ...)

    # --- Trust & attestation ---
    trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
    trust.add_argument("--manual-override", ...)
    trust.add_argument("--attested-external", ...)
    trust.add_argument("--attest", ...)

    # --- Post-processing ---
    post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
    post.add_argument("--merge", ...)
    post.add_argument("--similarity", ...)

    # --- Deprecated ---
    deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated (ignored)")
    deprecated.add_argument("--max-age", ...)
    deprecated.add_argument("--max-files", ...)
    deprecated.add_argument("--refresh", ...)
    deprecated.add_argument("--holistic", ...)
```

This transforms the flat wall of 40+ options into 6 clearly labeled sections when the user runs `desloppify review --help`.

#### 2C. Create Queue Renderer

**New file:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`

This is the core of Goal 2. It builds the full work queue and renders it as a compact table.

```python
"""Compact queue table renderer for `plan queue`."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json

from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue


def _truncate(text: str, max_len: int) -> str:
    """Truncate text to max_len, adding ellipsis if needed."""
    if len(text) <= max_len:
        return text
    return text[: max_len - 1] + "\u2026"


def _tier_color(tier: int) -> str:
    """Return color name for a tier label."""
    return {1: "red", 2: "yellow", 3: "cyan", 4: "dim"}.get(tier, "dim")


def _compact_rows(items: list[dict], plan: dict | None) -> list[list[str]]:
    """Build compact table rows from queue items."""
    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    for idx, item in enumerate(items, 1):
        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
        if kind == "cluster":
            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
            detector = item.get("detector", "")
            summary = _truncate(item.get("summary", ""), 50)
            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
            rows.append([
                str(idx),
                "--",
                detector,
                f"[{cluster_name}] {summary}",
                str(member_count),
                "cluster",
            ])
        else:
            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
            detector = item.get("detector", "")
            summary = _truncate(item.get("summary", ""), 50)
            cluster_info = ""
            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
            if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
                cluster_info = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
            skip_mark = ""
            if item.get("plan_skipped"):
                skip_kind = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temp")
                skip_mark = f" [{skip_kind[:4]}]"
            rows.append([
                str(idx),
                f"T{tier}",
                detector,
                summary + skip_mark,
                cluster_info,
                item.get("confidence", "med")[:3],
            ])
    return rows


def render_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Build and render the full compact queue."""
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return

    plan = load_plan()
    plan_data = plan if (
        plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters")
    ) else None

    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "compact")

    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            count=None,  # all items
            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
            status="open",
            include_subjective=True,
            no_tier_fallback=True,
            plan=plan_data,
            include_skipped=include_skipped,
            cluster=cluster_arg,
            collapse_clusters=not cluster_arg,
        ),
    )
    items = queue.get("items", [])
    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})

    if output_format == "json":
        # Reuse existing serializer
        from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts.output import serialize_item
        payload = {
            "command": "plan_queue",
            "items": [serialize_item(item) for item in items],
            "tier_counts": counts,
            "total": len(items),
        }
        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
        return

    if output_format == "md":
        from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts.output import render_markdown
        print(render_markdown(items))
        return

    # --- Compact terminal table ---
    total = len(items)
    t1, t2, t3, t4 = counts.get(1, 0), counts.get(2, 0), counts.get(3, 0), counts.get(4, 0)
    print(colorize(
        f"\n  Queue: {total} items  (T1:{t1} T2:{t2} T3:{t3} T4:{t4})",
        "bold",
    ))

    if plan_data and plan_data.get("active_cluster"):
        cn = plan_data["active_cluster"]
        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {cn}", "cyan"))

    skipped_count = len((plan_data or {}).get("skipped", {}))
    if skipped_count and not include_skipped:
        print(colorize(f"  ({skipped_count} skipped items hidden — use --include-skipped)", "dim"))

    if not items:
        print(colorize("  Queue is empty.", "green"))
        return

    print()
    rows = _compact_rows(items, plan_data)
    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster", "Conf"]
    widths = [4, 4, 18, 52, 18, 4]
    print_table(headers, rows, widths)
    print()
```

This renders output like:

```
  Queue: 47 items  (T1:3 T2:8 T3:28 T4:8)
  (5 skipped items hidden -- use --include-skipped)

#     Tier  Detector            Summary                                              Cluster             Conf
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1     --    unused              [auto/unused] Remove 12 unused findings               12                  cluster
2     --    logs                [auto/logs] Remove 8 console.log findings              8                   cluster
3     T1    security            SQL injection via string concatenation                 auto/security       hig
4     T1    security            Hardcoded API key in config.ts                         auto/security       hig
5     T2    smells              Async function with no await                                               med
...
```

#### 2D. Register in Command Registry (No Change Needed)

The `plan queue` subcommand is dispatched through `cmd_plan`, which already handles subcommand routing via `plan_action`. No change needed to `registry.py`.

---

### Implementation Sequence

1. **Start with missing help text** (1B) -- lowest risk, pure additive, every arg that lacks `help=` gets one.
2. **Restructure top-level epilog** (1A) -- single string replacement in `parser.py`.
3. **Add individual command epilogs** (1E) -- adding `epilog=` and `formatter_class=` to `add_parser()` calls.
4. **Review argument groups** (1D) -- restructure `_add_review_parser` using `add_argument_group()`. Functionally identical; only changes help output.
5. **Plan command description/epilog** (1C) -- add description and epilog to `_add_plan_parser`.
6. **Add `plan queue` subcommand** (2A-2C) -- parser registration, dispatch, and new renderer file. This is the only part with new functionality.
7. **Tests** -- update any tests that snapshot CLI help output. The existing test for `test_cmd_scan.py` and `test_cmd_show.py` may test parser construction and would need any parser-construction test assertions updated.

### Risks and Considerations

- **argparse `add_argument_group` for review:** When using argument groups, the group names appear as section headers in `--help` output. The arguments still belong to the same namespace. There is zero behavioral change -- only presentation.
- **`formatter_class` per subparser:** Each sub-parser that gets an epilog needs `formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter` passed explicitly. The parent parser already uses this, but subparsers do NOT inherit it.
- **`plan queue` reuses `build_work_queue`:** This is the same function `next` uses, so ordering and clustering behavior are already tested. The compact renderer is pure formatting.
- **No existing `add_argument_group` calls in the codebase:** This is a new pattern, but standard argparse. It will not conflict with anything.

### Critical Files for Implementation

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py` - All admin parser builders: plan description/epilog, review argument groups, detect/fix/viz/zone missing help text
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py` - Core command parsers: scan/status/tree/show/next/resolve/ignore missing help text and epilogs
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser.py` - Top-level parser and USAGE_EXAMPLES string to restructure
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py` - Plan command dispatcher: add "queue" action routing
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` - New file: compact queue table renderer for `plan queue`
